- **cognigy-agent-builder**: Builds a new Cognigy AI Agent end to end — discovers projects, ensures a working LLM (reusing one via packages before creating a new one), creates the agent, tests it, and refines its persona/job config. Use when the user wants to create, build, or set up a new AI agent from scratch. Runs the multi-step build loop in an isolated context and returns the agent id, endpoint, and a test summary. ([Cognigy/cognigy-plugin](https://github.com/Cognigy/cognigy-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/cognigy-agent-builder.md) · ★21)
- **cognigy-s2s-demo-builder**: Builds a speech-to-speech (S2S, also called voice-to-voice or V2V) demo AI Agent for a customer end to end. It checks that speech-to-speech is enabled, reuses or creates the gpt-live-1 voice model (GPT Live), creates and tests the agent, and publishes a branded click-to-call page. Use when the user wants a speech-to-speech, S2S, V2V or GPT Live voice demo from one prompt. Stops before writing anything when speech-to-speech is not enabled. Returns what it built and the demo link last. When you relay its result, call it speech-to-speech, also when the user said V2V or voice-to-voice. ([Cognigy/cognigy-plugin](https://github.com/Cognigy/cognigy-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/cognigy-s2s-demo-builder.md) · ★21)
- **cognigy-voice-go-live**: Runs the Voice Go-Live Checklist audit on a Cognigy voice AI agent in an isolated context and returns a short report — what fails, what is auto-fixable, and what stays manual. Use when the user wants a voice agent production-ready, a go-live audit, or voice settings validated or fixed. Read-only unless your prompt says the user approved applying fixes, and their backup choice. Pass the agent's id (agentId and projectId for an AI Agent, aiAgentId for a legacy one) if you have it. ([Cognigy/cognigy-plugin](https://github.com/Cognigy/cognigy-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/cognigy-voice-go-live.md) · ★21)

- **coherence-auditor**: Coherence auditor (goal mode). Audits each iteration's diff against the session blueprint (information architecture + data contract). Hard-fails only on objective rules — a contract value recomputed in a new code path, a contract value served from a non-canonical endpoint, or a new feature with no navigation path / a duplicate home for an existing entity. Subjective issues are advisory. Runs after the iteration's dispatch and before the goal-evaluator. ([dennisccy/incredible_auto_dev](https://github.com/dennisccy/incredible_auto_dev/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/coherence-auditor.md))
- **coherence-checker**: Use after code-builder and fact-verifier and BEFORE code-reviewer to check that the implementation structurally matches the plan, the spec it cites, and the verified facts — spec-to-code and plan-to-commit traceability, inverse-pair round-trip fidelity (decode(encode(x)) == x with no normalization tricks), cross-implementation parity, and contract/docstring fidelity. It is read-only and advisory: it reports structural mismatches with a loop-back target, but never edits, commits, or blocks. Run it on non-trivial changes (many files/commits, multiple phases, or work touching serialization, more… ([kevin-burns/claude-skills](https://github.com/kevin-burns/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/coherence-checker.md) · ★11)

- **cold-reviewer**: Zero-context code reviewer. Reviews changes cold - no spec, no project knowledge, no domain context. Catches what familiarity blinds you to. Delegate when you want a fresh-eyes pass before merging, or as part of a multi-perspective review. ([krzysztofsurdy/code-virtuoso](https://github.com/krzysztofsurdy/code-virtuoso/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · ★27)
- **cold-reviewer**: Cold diff review by ref. Resolves a commit SHA or base branch without intent framing, cites every finding as severity/claim/file:line, writes an incremental report, and never edits source. Use after gates on a behavior-bearing diff. ([ray-manaloto/dotfiles-legacy](https://github.com/ray-manaloto/dotfiles-legacy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · ★5)
- **cold-reviewer**: ../../agents/cold-reviewer.md ([p5ych0/watch-pr-skill](https://github.com/p5ych0/watch-pr-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **cold-reviewer**: Reads this branch's changes against the pull request's base cold, the way Codex and Copilot will, and reports what they would raise. Use before the first review request of a pull request and after each round's fixes. Read-only by instruction; it posts nothing. ([p5ych0/watch-pr-skill](https://github.com/p5ych0/watch-pr-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **cold-reviewer**: Performs cold-context single-plan critique when no separate requirements/spec file is available. Infers the problem from the plan itself and surfaces unchallenged assumptions, alternatives, and load-bearing bets. Use only when /review-plan determines no spec file exists. ([DavidRoyBlue/Autodidact](https://github.com/DavidRoyBlue/Autodidact/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **cold-reviewer**: Cold review of a pull request for /work-ticket, round 1 and the verify rounds after it. Fresh context, refutes by default, reports findings and edits nothing. ([matldupont/slipway](https://github.com/matldupont/slipway/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md))
- **cold-reviewer**: Review one generated Taku workspace using only local evidence ([Taku-OS/taku-publisher](https://github.com/Taku-OS/taku-publisher/blob/HEAD/packages/repo-to-stax-converter/benchmark/agents/kimi-cold-reviewer.md))
- **cold-reviewer**: Cold review of a PR or commit range it did not write, and re-review of the fix commits when resumed. Reports findings; never edits, commits or comments. ([tarka1939/My_Site](https://github.com/tarka1939/My_Site/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md))

- **cold-reviewer-strongest**: Cold review of a pull request for /work-ticket when a review named a risky surface (round 1 or a verify) or the diff changes a file the run is judged by, a gate file among them. Fresh context, refutes by default, reports findings and edits nothing. ([matldupont/slipway](https://github.com/matldupont/slipway/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer-strongest.md))
- **cold-scorer**: Adversarial cold-context evaluator. Spawn ONLY with (a) the diff or output artifact, (b) the manifest of what was claimed done, (c) test/lint/exit-code artifacts. Never give it the parent's conversation, reasoning, or excuses. Its output is the only valid source for ledger failure entries and skill-quality scores. ([ktg-one/AiA](https://github.com/ktg-one/AiA/blob/HEAD/sia-loop/agents/cold-scorer.md))
- **cold-solver**: Blind checker for Economics Academy content. Give it ONE temp file path and a mode - "solve" (stems + options, no answers: returns a letter and one-line reasoning per question), "verify" (flashcards + the notes page each must match: returns AGREE/DISAGREE per card), or "tag" (past-paper questions + a taxonomy excerpt: returns topic slugs per question). It never sees the answer key, the source data or a review sheet, so its result is an independent second opinion. ([eliotking01/economics-academy](https://github.com/eliotking01/economics-academy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-solver.md))
- **cold-start-auditor**: Run this agent periodically, or after any significant debugging session, to verify that the repo's knowledge record is lossless. It simulates an agent with zero conversational context — only the repo — and attempts to answer the questions a future maintainer would need answered. Failures identify exactly which knowledge is trapped in conversations or memory rather than in the durable record. ([clyde-tressler/semantic-compactor](https://github.com/clyde-tressler/semantic-compactor/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-start-auditor.md))
- **cold-start-author**: Usability test of austere-axi's agent-facing docs. Acts as an agent that has never seen the project, writes a review page for a given scenario from `--help` and `spec` alone, and reports where the docs failed it. Launch with a scenario that needs the changed component (without naming it), the repo path, and an output path outside the repo. ([MandalorianBatman/austere-axi](https://github.com/MandalorianBatman/austere-axi/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/cold-start-author.md) · ★8)
- **cold-stranger-ux**: Reacts to the running local site AS A STRANGER who has never heard of the project, just got a text link from a friend, and has a 2-minute mobile attention span. Use when the user asks "what does a normal person think of this", "is this confusing", "audit the UX", or after meaningful UI/copy changes land on local dev. Does NOT read CLAUDE.md, TODO.md, or any project docs. Drives a real browser via Playwright at iPhone-14 viewport, takes screenshots, reacts in plain English. Returns a punch list of bugs / confusion / would-bail moments per page. ([mikepsinn/optimitron](https://github.com/mikepsinn/optimitron/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-stranger-ux.md) · ★5)
- **cold-verifier**: Phase 11 cold verification agent that independently re-verifies CRITICAL and HIGH findings with zero prior context, following the adversarial review protocol to break residual confirmation bias from chamber debates ([vigolium/piolium](https://github.com/vigolium/piolium/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-verifier.md) · ★152)
- **cold-verifier**: Independently re-verify findings WITHOUT trusting prior analysis — an unbiased cold check to catch confirmation bias the chained reviewer/verifier path can introduce When NOT to use: routine correlation verification (verifier — faster and cheaper); first-pass review. ([baphuongna/pi-crew](https://github.com/baphuongna/pi-crew/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-verifier.md) · ★57)
- **cold-viewer**: 도식(다이어그램) 이미지를 대화 맥락 없이 처음 보는 사람으로서 보고, 처음 눈에 들어온 것과 메시지를 자기 말로 말하고, 그림이 주장하는 것으로 읽힌 관계와 막힌 곳을 표시한다. visualize-idea:visualize skill의 시청자 테스트 단계에서 쓴다. ([aumilkyeong/plugin-playground](https://github.com/aumilkyeong/plugin-playground/blob/HEAD/plugins/visualize-idea/agents/cold-viewer.md))

- **coldlight-component-author**: The design system has been over-layered (behaviors → dumb components → ui wrappers = 3 layers). No enterprise system does this. This plan establishes the correct pattern from the ground up. ([EonsofStupid/platform_devpulse](https://github.com/EonsofStupid/platform_devpulse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/coldlight-component-author.md))
- **coldlight-curator**: Zero-tolerance ColdLight design system enforcer. - **comment-ablation-auditor**: Adversarial reviewer of one comment-ablation diff. Reads the removed `#` blocks and the surviving ones in an assigned file group, re-derives each deleted fact's recoverability from the code alone, and reports either CLEAN-CUT (lossless) or DEEP-CUT (a fact the code cannot supply, with the keep class it should have survived under) — plus EARNED or UNEARNED for every surviving comment. Read-only: its tool set has no edit capability. Never reads the ablation's own reports, never invokes kata, never fixes anything. One-off, scoped to the ablation campaign's review wave. ([taylorsatula/mira](https://github.com/taylorsatula/mira/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/comment-ablation-auditor.md) · ★485)
- **comment-ablator**: Comment-ablation executor for one assignment of files. Reads the comment-ablation skill first, then deletes `#` comments the code already supplies, keeping only the four narrow classes, reporting contradictions and work orders instead of fixing them, and proving every file code-inert with the skill's gate. Reads a baseline before editing so a gate failure is attributable to its own change. One-off, scoped to the ablation campaign. Never touches string literals, docstrings, non-comment lines, or AGENTS.md maps; never commits. ([taylorsatula/mira](https://github.com/taylorsatula/mira/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/comment-ablator.md) · ★485)

Use before committing or opening a PR when a change touched comments. ([iengai/pbtb-rust](https://github.com/iengai/pbtb-rust/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/comment-reviewer.md))
- **comment-reviewer**: Reviews comments and docstrings for accuracy against the code they describe, covering both comments changed by a diff and — the primary case — comments left unchanged while the code beneath them changed. Expands each changed hunk to its enclosing declaration, cross-references the docstring against the current signature and behavior, and uses VCS history to separate stale comments from deliberate ones. Advisory only — never edits code or comments, and never reviews the code's correctness. ([jdh313/jdh-agents](https://github.com/jdh313/jdh-agents/blob/HEAD/marketplaces/claude/plugins/craft/agents/comment-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **comment-reviewer**: You review comments and docstrings for accuracy against the code they describe. You never touch code correctness, and you never edit anything — you return a report and the caller decides what to do with it. ([jdh313/jdh-agents](https://github.com/jdh313/jdh-agents/blob/HEAD/marketplaces/codex/plugins/craft/agents/comment-reviewer.md))
- **comment-reviewer**: karina:no-comments 가 이름으로 호출할 때만 쓴다(사용자 직접 호출, 또는 karina:implement 회차 끝에 그 스킬의 자동 모드). 지정 범위의 코드 주석을 지울 것·남길 것·리팩터링 대상(MUST KILL)·제약 주석으로 판정해 표로 낸다. 읽기 전용 — 파일을 고치지 않는다. 다른 상황에는 쓰지 않는다. ([jongcheol-pak/Karina-Plugins](https://github.com/jongcheol-pak/Karina-Plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude-harness-karina/agents/comment-reviewer.md))
- **comment-reviewer**: Inspect comments for redundant narration, stale claims and useful constraints; preserve uncertain meaning and keep cleanup scoped. ([logan-hunt/forge](https://github.com/logan-hunt/forge/blob/HEAD/agents/comment-reviewer.md))
- **comment-reviewer**: Applies the no-comments rule to a change. Finds every comment the change adds or touches, deletes it, writes any reason it held into the project's docs, and returns the names that should change so the code reads without it. Use before committing, or when asked to clean comments out of a diff or files. ([mtch3n/mskills](https://github.com/mtch3n/mskills/blob/HEAD/agents/comment-reviewer.md))
- **comment-reviewer**: Review comments and docs for claims that contradict behavior ([Zachshotamartin/just-vibe](https://github.com/Zachshotamartin/just-vibe/blob/HEAD/plugins/just-vibe/agents/comment-reviewer.md))
- **comment-reviewer-2**: Dragonfly-tuned comment reviewer. Use for the Phase 6 custom-review fan-out inside the dragonfly flow. One subagent is sufficient. Receives pre-collected context (changed files index, per-file diff files, optional initial-review log, ranked CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md chunks) injected automatically by the SubagentStart hook. You may, but do not need to, include additional guidance in the prompt. ([HalfVoxel/dragonfly](https://github.com/HalfVoxel/dragonfly/blob/HEAD/agents/comment-reviewer-2.md) · ★2)
- **comment-reviewer-3**: Dragonfly-tuned comment reviewer. Use for the Phase 6 custom-review fan-out inside the dragonfly flow. One subagent is sufficient. Receives pre-collected context (changed files index, per-file diff files, optional initial-review log, ranked CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md chunks) injected automatically by the SubagentStart hook. You may, but do not need to, include additional guidance in the prompt. ([HalfVoxel/dragonfly](https://github.com/HalfVoxel/dragonfly/blob/HEAD/agents/comment-reviewer-3.md) · ★2)
- **comment-reviewer-4**: Comment-reviewer control variant that runs WITHOUT the pre-collected SubagentStart context. It gets only its own prompt and must discover the PR itself (git diff, reading changed files). Used by scripts/compare-comment-reviewers.sh to measure how much the precomputed &lt;dragonfly-context&gt; actually buys over a cold reviewer. - **comment-rewriter**: Rewrites an implementer commit's comments to the comment standard — deletes what doesn't earn its place, fixes what survives, commits. Runs after every implementer commit. Edits directly; no findings. ([rnortman/claude-plugins](https://github.com/rnortman/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-chain/agents/comment-rewriter.md))
- **comment-rot-analyzer**: Read-only review of comments, docstrings, README snippets and TODOs a change made false — signatures, defaults, units, errors, side effects, safety claims, dead references; proposes corrected text ([RaNDoM6913/claude-code-superkit](https://github.com/RaNDoM6913/claude-code-superkit/blob/HEAD/packages/core/agents/comment-rot-analyzer.md) · ★3)
- **comment-rot-reviewer**: Inspects code comments and their nearby code to decide whether a comment is semantically stale (no longer matches the code). Use after edits, or when /comment-rot:scan needs Layer 3 semantic judgment. ([Bouwles/comment-rot-detector](https://github.com/Bouwles/comment-rot-detector/blob/HEAD/agents/comment-rot-reviewer.md))
- **comment-scan**: Advisory scan for unnecessary or over-cap comments, backing `swe-workbench:principle-clean-code`'s Comment discipline caps with a deterministic, checkable artifact instead of prose recall alone. **Advisory-with-accounting, not a hard gate** — the scan never fails your verify step; it produces findings that verdict accounting (below) requires you to account for before calling verify done.
